| MetaObject: reserved key names — "implementations MUST NOT make assumptions about values at these keys" | MUST NOT | ✅ compliant | src/meta.rs models _meta as open map; reserved keys touched only via named consts META_KEY_* (meta.rs:216-237); server passes non-reserved keys through opaquely (turul-mcp-server/src/handlers/mod.rs:99-118 extract_request_meta_extra) |
| _meta key grammar: prefix "MUST be labels separated by dots followed by slash"; name "MUST start and end with an alphanumeric"; reverse-DNS SHOULD — for keys the implementation emits | MUST | ✅ compliant | All framework-emitted keys are the schema-named io.modelcontextprotocol/* constants (meta.rs:216-237, RequestMetaObject serde renames); wire shapes asserted in tests/compliance.rs meta tests — *Emission side only; no general grammar validator for third-party keys — receivers are not required to validate, see extensions row for the one place validation is arguably owed.* |
| RequestMetaObject io.modelcontextprotocol/protocolVersion Required; "MUST match the MCP-Protocol-Version header; otherwise the server MUST return a 400 Bad Request" | MUST | ✅ compliant | turul-http-mcp-server/src/streamable_http.rs:1599-1640 (body-vs-header mismatch and missing _meta version → 400); tests turul-mcp-server/tests/discover_stateless_2026.rs::header_body_protocol_version_mismatch_is_rejected_with_32020, ::missing_meta_is_rejected_with_invalid_params — *Schema mandates only HTTP 400; the -32020 HeaderMismatch JSON-RPC code on the mismatch path is framework-chosen (SEP-2243), not schema-defined — acceptable.* |
| "If the server does not support the requested version, it MUST return an UnsupportedProtocolVersionError" — code -32022, data {supported, requested}, HTTP status "MUST be 400 Bad Request" | MUST | ✅ compliant | streamable_http.rs:1360-1389 (400 + -32022 with supported/requested); protocol mapping src/lib.rs:482-489; test discover_stateless_2026.rs::unsupported_protocol_version_header_is_rejected_with_32022 (asserts code -32022 at :297) |
| RequestMetaObject io.modelcontextprotocol/clientInfo — "Required" on every request | MUST | 🟡 partial | Required-field extraction enforced on the 2026 path (streamable_http.rs:1599-1615 required-_meta gate; handlers/mod.rs per-request meta); whole-_meta-absent rejection tested (discover_stateless_2026.rs::missing_meta_is_rejected_with_invalid_params) but no dedicated test for _meta present with clientInfo specifically absent (only clientCapabilities-absent has one) — *Implementation appears correct; the clientInfo-absent edge is untested — see gap G2.* | — **RE-GRADED 2026-06-12** (post-P2-batch verification): crates/turul-http-mcp-server/src/streamable_http.rs:1670 validates clientInfo presence (required-field extraction gate). Test incomplete_meta_branches_are_rejected (wire_edges_2026.rs:223-250) covers missing clientInfo and asserts -32602. However, no dedicated isolated test for clientInfo-absent when protocolVersion and clientCapabilities are present (only group test).
| io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities Required per request; "Servers MUST NOT infer capabilities from prior requests" | MUST NOT | ✅ compliant | Per-request gate in handlers/mod.rs:62-80 (caps read from THIS request's _meta only; stateless, no cached negotiation); tests discover_stateless_2026.rs::incomplete_meta_missing_client_capabilities_is_rejected and mrtr_2026.rs::undeclared_capability_is_rejected_with_32021, ::resources_read_capability_gate_applies |
| io.modelcontextprotocol/logLevel: "If absent, the server MUST NOT send any notifications/message notifications for this request"; declared level is the threshold | MUST NOT | ✅ compliant | tests turul-mcp-server/tests/log_gating_2026.rs::message_notifications_require_a_declared_log_level (:138) and ::declared_level_is_the_severity_threshold (:165), real-HTTP suite |
| Result.resultType: "Servers implementing this protocol version MUST include this field" | MUST | ✅ compliant | result_type is a non-Option field on every Result-shaped type (e.g. tools.rs:305, 497; discover.rs; input_required.rs) and ResultType::Serialize always emits (src/result_type.rs:63-67); wire-shape tests across tests/compliance.rs |
| "when a client receives a result from a server implementing an earlier protocol version (which does not include resultType), the client MUST treat the absent field as 'complete'" | MUST | ✅ compliant | ResultType::default() == Complete + #[serde(default)] at every embedding site (result_type.rs:38-43); 9 back-compat tests, e.g. tests/compliance.rs::complete_result_back_compat_accepts_missing_result_type (:608), ::call_tool_result_back_compat_accepts_missing_result_type (:1578), src/discover.rs:320 |
| ResultType open union ("complete" \| "input_required" \| string) — unknown discriminators tolerated and round-tripped | INFO | ✅ compliant | ResultType::Other(String) with hand-written Serialize/Deserialize (result_type.rs:27-77); tests accepts_unknown_discriminator_as_other / unknown_result_type_value_is_preserved_as_other per ADR-027 rev-log 2026-06-07 |
| Error.message "SHOULD be limited to a concise single sentence" | SHOULD | 🧪 untested | McpError Display strings in protocol src/lib.rs are single-sentence by convention; no test or lint enforces it — *Stylistic SHOULD; no action proposed.* |
| MissingRequiredClientCapabilityError: code MISSING_REQUIRED_CLIENT_CAPABILITY = -32021, data.requiredCapabilities; "For HTTP, the response status code MUST be 400 Bad Request" | MUST | ✅ compliant | src/lib.rs:475-480 (-32021 + {requiredCapabilities}); HTTP mapping streamable_http.rs:2115-2133 (-32021/-32020 → 400); test mrtr_2026.rs::undeclared_capability_is_rejected_with_32021 asserts BOTH status 400 and code -32021 |
| InputRequiredResult: "At least one of inputRequests or requestState MUST be present" | MUST | ✅ compliant | Protocol-level deserialize validation src/input_required.rs:197-250; server construction guard handlers/mod.rs:88-94 (neither present → error, never emitted); MRTR round-trip tests mrtr_2026.rs::mrtr_round_trip_completes_the_original_call, ::mrtr_round_trip_on_resources_read, ::mrtr_round_trip_on_prompts_get |
| CancelledNotification: requestId "MUST correspond to the ID of a request previously issued in the same direction"; notification MAY arrive late; "any associated processing SHOULD cease" | SHOULD | 🟡 partial | Type compliant: CancelledNotificationParams.request_id is Option<RequestId> per schema (ADR-027 Slice A' A3; src/notifications.rs). Send side only: turul-http-mcp-server/src/notification_bridge.rs:85. No 2026-path handler that receives notifications/cancelled and ceases in-flight processing, and no test — *SHOULD-level behavioral gap — see gap G1.* |
| server/discover: "Servers MUST implement server/discover. Clients MAY call it" | MUST | ✅ compliant | Server handler wired in turul-mcp-server/src/server.rs + lib.rs; test discover_stateless_2026.rs::server_discover_answers_without_a_session (:78); client implements the MAY in turul-mcp-client/src/protocol/v2026_07_28.rs |
| ClientCapabilities.extensions / ServerCapabilities.extensions: "Keys MUST follow the _meta key naming rules, with a mandatory prefix" | MUST | 🟡 partial | Maps modeled as Option<HashMap<String, Value>> (src/initialize.rs:182, :280) — wire shape correct, but no grammar/mandatory-prefix validation on keys; no test exercises an invalid key — *Gap G4 (P2). The framework neither emits extension keys today nor validates inbound ones.* |
| Icons: clients that render icons "MUST support at least image/png, image/jpeg" and "SHOULD also support image/svg+xml, image/webp" | MUST | ➖ n/a | Requirement targets icon-RENDERING UI clients; turul-mcp-client is a headless library and renders no icons. Icon type itself is modeled (src/icons.rs) with compliance wire tests |
| Icon security: "Consumers SHOULD take steps to ensure URLs serving icons are from the same domain"; "SHOULD take appropriate precautions when consuming SVGs" | SHOULD | ➖ n/a | Same justification — no icon fetching/rendering surface ships in this repo |
| CacheableResult: ttlMs (required, @minimum 0) and cacheScope (required, "public"\|"private") on cacheable results incl. DiscoverResult; ttl semantics (0 = immediately stale, positive = fresh window) | MUST | ✅ compliant | Required (non-Option) fields, e.g. tools.rs:314-317 ('REQUIRED on the wire' tools.rs:296), src/caching.rs:72-76; DiscoverResult extends CacheableResult per 2026-06-07 re-pin (ADR-027 rev-log #3, src/discover.rs); server tests discover_stateless_2026.rs::resources_list_dispatches_statelessly_with_cacheable_result (:185), ::prompts_list_dispatches_statelessly_with_cacheable_result (:205) — *ADR-027 'Phase 7 finalization — tighten transitional Option cache fields' is closed for these result types: fields are required, not Option.* |
| cacheScope "private": "Shared caches (e.g., multi-tenant gateways) MUST NOT serve a cached copy to a different user"; client MAY cache per ttlMs | MUST NOT | ➖ n/a | turul-mcp-client implements no response cache and ships no shared/gateway cache, so the MUST NOT is vacuously satisfied; the MAY-level client-side caching feature is simply not implemented — *If client-side caching is ever added, this row becomes a live requirement.* |
| subscriptions/listen SubscriptionFilter: "the server MUST NOT send notification types the client has not explicitly requested" | MUST NOT | ✅ compliant | tests turul-mcp-server/tests/subscriptions_listen_2026.rs::listen_acks_first_then_delivers_only_requested_types (:183), ::listen_ack_omits_unsupported_types (:342), ::listen_requires_sse_accept (:317), ::resources_subscribe_capability_is_advertised_truthfully (:407) |
| Subscription ID: "The client MUST treat this ID as an opaque value" | MUST | ✅ compliant | Client extracts io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId verbatim and never parses it (turul-mcp-client/src/client.rs:1062-1066, :1865); MRTR requestState similarly round-tripped opaquely (tests/e2e_2026_real_server.rs:143,197 — 'st-9' echoed verbatim) |
| CallToolResult: "Any errors that originate from the tool SHOULD be reported inside the result object" (isError), not as protocol-level errors | SHOULD | 🟡 partial | CallToolResult.is_error is modeled and tools may return it; but the framework maps McpTool::call Err(McpError) to a JSON-RPC error (handlers/mod.rs:91 McpError::ToolExecutionError path), per the deliberate AGENTS.md §Release Readiness Notes 'Tool Error Propagation' policy — *Gap G5 (P2) — deliberate framework stance vs schema SHOULD; disposition belongs to the Tools-section audit, recorded here because the statement is on this page.* |
| Sampling (deprecated SEP-2577) client-as-sampler MUSTs: "client MUST return an error if [tools/toolChoice] is provided but ClientCapabilities.sampling.tools is not declared"; toolChoice 'required'/'none' model behavior; "client MUST evaluate [model hints] in order"; preferences MAY be ignored | MUST | ➖ n/a | These bind a client that executes LLM sampling. turul-mcp-client ships no sampler — sampling InputRequests are surfaced to the embedding application (InputRequest::CreateMessage, src/input_required.rs), which owns enforcement. Types model all fields (src/sampling.rs) — *Server side of the same area IS covered: undeclared sampling capability → -32021 MissingRequiredClientCapability (handlers/mod.rs `validate_input_request_capabilities`).* |

Field modeled with the deprecation documented in src/sampling.rs (SEP-2577/SEP-2596 sweep per ADR-027 rev-log) |
| ToolUseContent/ToolResultContent: "Clients SHOULD preserve this field [_meta]"; ToolResultContent.id "MUST match the ID from a previous ToolUseContent" | SHOULD | ➖ n/a | Binds sampling-loop clients (LLM hosts); not shipped here. Both content variants carry _meta and id losslessly at the type level (src/content.rs ToolUse/ToolResult variants, compliance wire tests) |

- [x] **SCHEMA/G1** — No 2026-path handling of inbound notifications/cancelled — **CLOSED 2026-06-11** (accept-and-ignore: `notifications/cancelled` is now an explicitly registered notification (202, never 404 — wire test notes the 202 contract pre-existed via the transport's fire-and-forget notification path); cancellation of in-flight work on Streamable HTTP is the stream-close mechanism (982667fb), and cross-request correlation by id is impossible without sessions on the stateless lane — "Invalid cancellation notifications SHOULD be ignored")
  - Requirement: "This notification indicates that the result will be unused, so any associated processing SHOULD cease." — CancelledNotification, https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/schema (schema.ts CancelledNotificationParams doc)
  - Current: Only the send side exists (crates/turul-http-mcp-server/src/notification_bridge.rs:85). Inbound notifications/cancelled is accepted as a generic notification; no code cancels the referenced in-flight request, and no test exercises it (grep across turul-mcp-server/src and turul-http-mcp-server/src finds no receive-side handler).
  - Fix: crates/turul-mcp-server (notification dispatch) + a real-HTTP test alongside tests/*_2026.rs; SHOULD-level, governed by ADR-027 (stateless core) — confirm intended scope before building, per AGENTS.md §Complexity Control
- [x] **SCHEMA/G2** — Missing dedicated rejection test for _meta present but clientInfo absent — **FIXED 2026-06-11 (covered by `incomplete_meta_branches_are_rejected`)**
  - Requirement: "io.modelcontextprotocol/clientInfo: Implementation — Identifies the client software making the request. Required." — RequestMetaObject, https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/schema
  - Current: Whole-_meta-absent and clientCapabilities-absent are tested (crates/turul-mcp-server/tests/discover_stateless_2026.rs::missing_meta_is_rejected_with_invalid_params :243, ::incomplete_meta_missing_client_capabilities_is_rejected :257); the clientInfo-absent permutation has no test. Implementation appears to reject it (streamable_http.rs:1599-1615 required-_meta extraction) but is unproven.
  - Fix: crates/turul-mcp-server/tests/discover_stateless_2026.rs — add the third permutation; CLAUDE.md §Test Coverage Discipline #4 (revert-and-fail) applies
- [x] **SCHEMA/G3** — EXAMPLES_PIN.md 'Captured: 2026-05-24' date stale after the 2026-06-10 pin advance — **FIXED 2026-06-11 (EXAMPLES_PIN.md Captured date corrected to the 2026-06-10 pin advance)**
  - Requirement: Pin provenance accuracy — ADR-027 revision log 2026-06-10 records 'Fixture pin advanced to 1304c8fe via refresh --write' (docs/adr/027-targeting-mcp-draft-2026-v1.md)
  - Current: crates/turul-mcp-protocol-2026-07-28/schema/EXAMPLES_PIN.md:9 says 'Captured: 2026-05-24' while carrying SHA 1304c8fe (a 2026-06-09 upstream commit; verified == upstream main HEAD, compare ahead_by: 0). The SHA is current — only the regenerated doc's date field is wrong, suggesting the refresh tool does not update it.
  - Fix: crates/turul-mcp-protocol-2026-07-28/src/bin (refresh tool that regenerates EXAMPLES_PIN.md) — fix the Captured field emission; governed by ADR-027 regeneration workflow. NOT a re-pin trigger.
- [x] **SCHEMA/G5** — Tool-originating errors surface as JSON-RPC errors, not in-result isError — **DISPOSITIONED 2026-06-11 (deliberate, AGENTS.md-documented stance): the framework contract is `Err(McpError)` = protocol-level error, `Ok(CallToolResult::error(...))` = model-visible tool error with isError — tools choose which surface fits; auto-converting Err to isError would hide infrastructure failures from operators. The SHOULD is dischargeable per-tool today**
  - Requirement: "Any errors that originate from the tool SHOULD be reported inside the result object, with isError set to true, not as an MCP protocol-level error response." — CallToolResult, https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/schema
  - Current: McpTool::call Err(McpError) propagates as a JSON-RPC error (crates/turul-mcp-server/src/handlers/mod.rs:91 ToolExecutionError path). This is a deliberate, documented framework stance (AGENTS.md §Release Readiness Notes 'Tool Error Propagation': never re-wrap as successful CallToolResult::error payloads). Tools CAN return isError results themselves; the type is modeled.
  - Fix: Disposition decision, not necessarily code: either document the SHOULD-deviation in crates/turul-mcp-protocol-2026-07-28/COMPLIANCE.md, or change handler mapping in crates/turul-mcp-server/src/handlers/mod.rs — owned by the Tools-section audit; conflicts with the AGENTS.md policy must be surfaced to
#### Promoted 2026-06-12 (post-re-grade pass)

- [x] **VER-4** — Headerless `initialize` rejection (400 + -32020) does not name supported protocol versions — **FIXED 2026-06-12** (`headerless_initialize_rejection_names_supported_versions`, red-phase recorded: pre-fix body had no `data`)
  - Requirement: "A server that supports only modern versions SHOULD name the protocol versions it supports in any error it returns to an initialize request, on any transport" — https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/basic/versioning §Backward Compatibility
  - Current: the headed-`initialize` 404 path carries `error.data.supported` (streamable_http.rs, `initialize` special-case in the unknown-method branch — RE-GRADED row), but a true legacy client sends `initialize` WITHOUT `MCP-Protocol-Version` and hits the missing-header rejection, whose -32020 is built with `data: None`.
  - Fix: crates/turul-http-mcp-server/src/streamable_http.rs — when the header-validation failure is the missing-version case and the body method is `initialize`, attach `data.supported`; wire test in mcp_headers_2026.rs, revert-and-fail recorded.
- [x] **PAT/G10** — `notifications/cancelled` reason is not extracted into the cancellation log line — **FIXED 2026-06-12** (`CancelledNotificationHandler` extracts `requestId` + `reason` into a structured log line; 3 unit tests on the extraction incl. numeric ids)
  - Requirement: "Both parties SHOULD log cancellation reasons for debugging" — https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/basic/patterns/cancellation
  - Current: the method is a registered notification (202, never 404 — PAT/G6 slice) and the generic notification handler logs the raw params at info, which incidentally includes `reason`; nothing extracts `requestId`/`reason` as the debuggable cancellation record the SHOULD describes.
  - Fix: crates/turul-mcp-server/src/handlers/mod.rs — dedicated cancelled-notification arm extracting `requestId` + `reason` into a structured log line; unit test on the extraction.
- [x] **CF/GAP-CF-8** — No elicitation response-vs-requested-schema validation helper on either side — **FIXED 2026-06-12** (`turul_mcp_builders::validate_elicit_content` covering required/unknown keys, primitive types, string-length + numeric bounds, integer-ness, enum membership across the union shapes; 6 unit tests; wired into the MRTR example's retry leg and live-verified; format assertions annotation-only by design)
  - Requirement: Form security: "Clients SHOULD validate all responses against the provided schema" and "Servers SHOULD validate received data matches the requested schema" — https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/client/elicitation §Security
  - Current: the server hands raw `InputResponse` content to the tool unvalidated; on the stateless 2026 lane the framework CANNOT enforce this centrally — the leg-1 requested schema is not retained anywhere server-side (no session), so only the tool (which re-derives the schema) can validate the leg-2 content.
  - Fix: validation helper `validate_elicit_content(&ElicitationSchema, &Value)` covering the primitive form-schema surface (string/number/integer/boolean/enum incl. legacy titled shape, required keys, unknown-key rejection) in crates/turul-mcp-builders; used by the MRTR example; unit tests. Framework-central enforcement documented as impossible-by-design on the stateless lane.
- [x] **BP-5** — No published statement of supported JSON Schema dialects — **FIXED 2026-06-12 (COMPLIANCE.md §"Supported JSON Schema dialects": 2020-12 emission posture + explicit no-validating-processor statement; discharges the SHOULD, which asks for documentation)**
  - Requirement: "Clients and servers SHOULD document which schema dialects they support" — https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/basic §Schema Dialects
  - Current (pre-fix): no dialect-support statement existed in crate READMEs or COMPLIANCE.md.
  - Fix: crates/turul-mcp-protocol-2026-07-28/COMPLIANCE.md (docs-only).
- [x] **UTIL/COMP-3** — Completion relevance-ranking / fuzzy-matching / rate-limiting SHOULDs unimplemented — **DISPOSITIONED 2026-06-12: ranking and fuzzy matching are provider semantics — `McpCompletion` providers own suggestion content and order (the framework preserves provider order and applies the 100-cap + total/hasMore); rate limiting is the middleware layer's job per the PAT/G9 disposition (ADR-012). Input validation (the fourth SHOULD in the row) is already enforced: typed params → -32602 incl. ref-literal validation**
  - Requirement: "Servers SHOULD return suggestions sorted by relevance, implement fuzzy matching, rate limit completion requests, validate all inputs" — https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/server/utilities/completion
  - Current: provider routing + typed-param validation landed 2026-06-11 (UTIL/COMP-1/2); the remaining SHOULDs are application/provider-level concerns.
  - Fix: disposition only; provider-responsibility documented in `CompletionHandler` rustdoc.

## E2E test plan

The release gate is the real-HTTP, production-path suite (Builder → `server.handler()` →
wire bytes) per CLAUDE.md §Test Coverage Discipline. Lanes (all wired into
`.github/workflows/ci.yml` + `scripts/ci-gates.sh`):

| Lane | Command | Covers |
|---|---|---|
| Default 2026 server wire | `cargo test -p turul-mcp-server --no-default-features --features http,sse,protocol-2026-07-28` | `discover_stateless_2026`, `stateless_2026_http_surface`, `subscriptions_listen_2026`, `mcp_headers_2026`, `error_mapping_2026`, `mrtr_2026`, `mcp_param_2026`, `log_gating_2026`, `schema_fidelity_2026` |
| Bilingual client e2e | `cargo test -p turul-mcp-client` | negotiation (`bilingual_negotiation`), 2026 ops vs wiremock (`bilingual_2026_operations`), real-server e2e incl. MRTR + listen (`e2e_2026_real_server`) |
| Protocol crate vs pinned schema | `cargo test -p turul-mcp-protocol-2026-07-28 --features compliance` | type/wire-shape fidelity against vendored `schema.ts` |
| 2025-11-25 opt-in regression | `scripts/ci-gates.sh` `gate_opt_in_2025` (per-crate matrix) | stateful lane: handshake, sessions, GET SSE, tasks |
| Spec mutex | `gate_mutex` | ADR-029 `compile_error!` on dual protocol features |
| Docs | `cargo fmt --check`, `RUSTDOCFLAGS="-D warnings" cargo doc --no-deps` | doc health |

**Coverage debt** (from the tables above; tracked in the gap register): every 🧪 row is an
implemented behavior with no test — the P1 test-only items are VER-1 (-32022 fallback wire
path), AUTH-1 (OAuth 401/PRM on the 2026 transport), and TOOLS-G1 (client x-mcp-header tool
exclusion). New suites land in the same lanes table above; a suite is "done" when its
revert-and-fail check is recorded in the commit message.

## Deployment

The 2026-07-28 stateless core changes the deployment posture (see
the prior release-readiness review for history — deleted in the 0.4 docs purge, see git history; this section is forward-looking):

**Lambda (primary serverless target — `turul-mcp-aws-lambda`)**
- Stateless requests need no session storage, no DynamoDB session table, and no sticky
  routing: every POST is self-contained (`_meta` carries version/identity/capabilities).
- `subscriptions/listen` is a long-lived POST SSE response — on Lambda this is bounded by
  the invocation timeout and API Gateway's 29s idle limit; deployments needing long-lived
  subscriptions should route `/mcp` listen traffic to an EC2/container tier or use
  Lambda response streaming with reconnect-on-close clients (stream state is request-scoped
  by design: clients re-issue `subscriptions/listen` after a drop).
- OAuth: `turul-mcp-oauth` validates bearer JWTs in-process (RFC 9728 PRM + RFC 9207 iss);
  API Gateway authorizers remain supported via `x-authorizer-*` extraction.
- Task support (2025 lane / future SEP-2663 extension crate) still requires
  `.with_task_storage()` with a durable backend (DynamoDB).
- Dispatcher method-registration parity (2026-07-13): `turul-mcp-aws-lambda`'s registered
  method set now mirrors the non-Lambda `McpServerBuilder` authority per feature lane —
  `server/discover` (2026 lane) and `notifications/cancelled` (both lanes) added; `ping`,
  `roots/list`, `notifications/message`, `notifications/progress`, `notifications/roots/*`
  gated to `protocol-2025-11-25`; the unconditional `completion/complete` mock removed. A
  crate-private `registered_methods()` parity probe plus wire tests pin the registered set
  per lane against an explicit spec-derived expectation. See ADR-001 / ADR-029 revision logs
  (2026-07-13).

**EC2 / containers (long-lived servers)**
- Same binary serves both: default build = 2026 stateless; `--no-default-features
  --features protocol-2025-11-25` = stateful 2025 line (single-spec per build, ADR-029).
- `subscriptions/listen` streams are held in-process; scale-out needs either sticky routing
  for listen connections or acceptance of re-subscribe-on-rebalance (spec-sanctioned).
- Origin validation / DNS-rebinding protection is ON by default (`OriginPolicy::SameOriginOrLoopback`,
  ADR-031); cross-origin browser deployments must configure `AllowList`, and deployments that
  enforce origin upstream (API Gateway / ALB) may set `Disabled`.

**Version negotiation in mixed fleets**
- The bilingual client (`turul-mcp-client`, ADR-030) probes `server/discover` and falls back
  to 2025 `initialize` on `-32601`/`-32602`, or on `-32022` whose data.supported names 2025-11-25, so rolling a fleet from 2025-11-25 to
  2026-07-28 does not require client lockstep.

## Maintenance

- **This file is the single driver.** New spec work starts by updating the relevant table
  row(s) and gap entries here; the CHANGELOG references slices, ADRs record decisions.
- Re-audit triggers: upstream schema re-pin (ADR-027 §regeneration triggers), a new spec
  page in the ToC, or a release-readiness review.
- The prior driver (the 2026-06-10 release-readiness review) is historical
  context as of 2026-06-11; it is superseded by this document for open work.
